A simulated user holds a hidden strategy sampled from a compositional grammar and reveals it only when asked. On the 46 real-world tasks of our this http URL platform, Claude Opus 5.5 with Claude Code and GPT-6.1 Sol with Codex complete 9 each on their own and 39 and 35 when connected to HEELO through our MCP server, with 1.9$\times$ and 2.3$\times$ less time and 1.55$\times$ lower cost per completed task.

68. 【2611.07453】Latency-Aware Adaptive Best-of-$N$

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07453

作者：Alon Jacobson,Qian Xie,Peter I. Frazier

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：improves language model, spending additional computation, language model responses, Test-time compute, Adaptive TTC methods

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Test-time compute (TTC) improves language model responses by spending additional computation at inference time, and the right amount of compute depends on the prompt. Adaptive TTC methods allocate compute based on signals of response quality such as observed rewards, often by stopping once the signal exceeds a fixed threshold, and generally measure cost by the number of generations or tokens. But in online serving, latency is also a cost: it grows with server load, so a method using a fixed threshold spends too little compute when generation is fast and too much when it is slow. For Best-of-$N$ sampling, a widely used TTC method, we propose a two-stage adaptive algorithm that draws an initial batch, then uses the observed rewards and the batch's latency to decide whether to draw a second batch and at what size by comparing expected improvement against predicted cost: it draws one when the first batch's best reward is below a threshold that decreases with the first batch's latency, without explicitly measuring server load. We map each reward to its value under the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of the prompt's reward distribution, so the same tradeoff parameters can be reused across tasks; a regression fitted offline on other prompts predicts a distribution for the CDF value of the first batch's best reward, giving the expected improvement in closed form. We evaluate the method by simulation on a preference-based alignment benchmark, measuring generations-equivalent cost: generations plus latency, converted to generations. The method reaches Best-of-$N$'s reward at lower generations-equivalent cost, and conditioning on the measured first-batch latency saves at least as much of that cost as fixed-threshold (latency-unaware) baselines, and more when latency is highly variable, when it grows with batch size, and when the second batch's latency is highly predictable from the first's.

69. 【2611.07372】EnvOpt: Automated Optimization for Agent Environments

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07372

作者：Mingxuan Li,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Hanchen Li,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alex Dimakis

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：LLM agent cost, LLM agent, cost, LLM, stationary environments

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:An LLM agent's cost and success depend on its model, its harness, and its environment. In addition to the widely studied directions of parameter tuning and harness optimization, we propose the third direction: optimization over stationary environments. Agents are often deployed in rich environments shared by many tasks, such as a company's Slack workspace or a complex collection of data sources, documents or tickets. We call these environments stationary environments. We introduce EnvOpt, a method for automated end-to-end optimization of stationary environments for any given model and harness, using real or synthetic tasks without needing any graders or reference answers. On AutomationBench, EnvOpt reduces the cost of Claude Haiku 4.5 by 72.7% and raises its pass rate by 14.34%. The same environment transfers to Opus 5, cutting its cost by 68.1% with a 1.34% gain. Added on top of Meta-Harness's optimized harness for Haiku 4.5, it lowers cost by a further 83.4% and adds 11.17% accuracy over Meta-Harness alone. Across 5 benchmarks, EnvOpt significantly reduces the cost of agents while improving or maintaining pass rate, and is transferable to different models and different harnesses. We also discuss common successful and rejected environment optimization strategies during automated optimization. More broadly, our results show that how information is represented to an agent shapes both its cost and its success, making the environment worth optimizing in addition to the parameters and the harness.

70. 【2611.07283】Encoding and Propagation of Grammatical Number in Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07283

作者：Jacob Solinsky(1),Serguei Pakhomov(1) ((1) University of Minnesota)

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：translating Large Language, Large Language Model, Sparse autoencoders, translating Large, Large Language

备注： 50 pages, 19 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become an increasingly popular means of translating Large Language Model (LLM) hidden state vectors into a human-comprehensible list of features or symbols. As such, SAEs may provide insight into an LLM's knowledge representation and internal ``mental'' grammar - topics of pragmatic interest for mechanistic interpretation of AI behavior and of theoretical interest for the study of in silico representations of human language. Language models with fewer than 100M parameters, trained on small corpora, nonetheless exhibit strong grammatical competence and discourse coherence, despite producing content of limited reliability. We use SAEs that we trained on one such model (Pythia-70M) to conduct an in-depth examination of how a single grammatical phenomenon, number agreement, is represented and processed in an LLM. We demonstrate the mechanism and the methodology by which SAEs can be used to find and propagate ``plural noun'', ``plural matrix subject'', and ``generate a plural verb'' features which cause the LLM to produce plural verb forms such as ``are'', ``do'', or ``have'' instead of ``is'', ``does'', and ``has'' in response to prompts such as ``the men in the park''. We show that intervening to remove these features from the hidden states of plural subject noun tokens causes the model to produce singular verbs instead, and vice versa. We also show that corresponding ``singular noun'' and ``generate a singular verb'' features are not as effective for this task. This asymmetry suggests that Pythia-70m makes use of a privative representation of grammatical number in English. These findings replicated with larger LLMs Gemma-2-2b and Llama-3.1-8b yielded similar results. Our main contribution is a novel approach to the use of SAEs to understanding how LLMs encode human language that addresses a number of technical and methodological challenges.

71. 【2611.07263】HyFlash: A Prefix-Memory Prior for On-Device Speculative Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07263

作者：Jongwoo Ko,Pashmina Cameron

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：single backbone pass, block drafting accelerates, drafting accelerates speculative, accelerates speculative decoding, predicting multiple future

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Parallel block drafting accelerates speculative decoding by predicting multiple future positions in a single backbone pass, but on-device deployment constrains both drafter capacity and context storage. Under tight memory budgets, drafters must model long-range dependencies and fine-grained draft-position interactions without the growing KV-cache overhead of full-context attention. We propose HyFlash, a compact parallel drafter that introduces a prefix-memory prior to separate long-range context modeling from fine-grained draft-position interactions with limited capacity. Local attention captures recent context and draft-position interactions, while recurrent memory compresses historical target features into fixed-size states. This inductive bias enables effective local and long-range modeling with bounded draft-side memory. Experiments show that HyFlash achieves higher acceptance than full-context attention with substantially less KV storage and with larger gains for shallow drafters and long contexts.

72. 【2611.07241】$D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241

作者：Bowen Fang(Columbia University),Dingmin Wang(AWS AI Lab),Hengzhi Pei(Amazon AGI)

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：On-policy distillation, teacher next-token distributions, student-generated prefixes, model to match, next-token distributions

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Extrapolative OPD further extends the teacher's log-probability shift relative to a reference policy to construct stronger learning targets. However, existing methods couple extrapolation direction and distance, allowing the magnitude of the teacher's shift to dictate how far the target moves. We propose $D^2$-OPD, decoupling these two factors: a per-prefix hard KL constraint bounds the target's distance from the teacher, while an adaptive combination of the teacher's post-training shift and the initial student--teacher gap determines the extrapolation direction. We optimize the student toward this target using forward KL, providing learning signals even for low-probability tokens. We evaluate three settings: where standard OPD performs well, where it struggles with base-model initialization, and where no teacher reference is available. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, $D^2$-OPD attains the highest score in all three settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.45 points. Ablations validate the contributions of each component.

73. 【2611.07238】How Do AI Agents Waste Your Money?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07238

作者：Huimin Zeng,Yuxuan Lu,Jesse Cai,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Di Wu,Po-Nien Kung,Wenbo Hu,Ziyi Wang,Dakuo Wang,Jiaxin Pei,Kai-Wei Chang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：consume massive tokens, consume massive, wide range, token, token waste

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI agents may consume massive tokens across a wide range of tasks, but how many of those tokens are really necessary? Although prior work has shown that agents can incur unexpectedly high token costs, we still lack a systematic way to identify and measure token waste. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for measuring token waste across heterogeneous agent systems. Our framework records and normalizes complete agent trajectories, identifies removable spans using an LLM-as-a-Judge (LLMaJ), and maps the identified text spans back to the original trajectory for token-level measurement. We validate the identified waste through direct interventions by removing marked spans, resuming agent execution, and measuring changes in task outcomes and subsequent actions. In online pruning experiments, we remove 41-46% of the model's reasoning while largely preserving task performance across two distinct agent benchmarks. Using this framework, we analyze agents across multiple settings. Our analysis shows that token waste has a system-level structure that aggregate token usage alone cannot reveal. First, waste varies more across tasks and agent settings than across models. Second, reasoning and visible execution can exhibit different waste patterns. Third, different harnesses can introduce distinct sources of inefficiency even under the same model and task. Overall, our framework transforms token waste into an actionable optimization signal, enabling researchers and practitioners to train more efficient models, design better reasoning strategies, and optimize agent harnesses toward reducing unnecessary computation rather than merely reducing token usage.

74. 【2611.07234】Archetypal contexts in the neurons of language models: an architecture for intelligence?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07234

作者：David Nordfors

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：model holds relational, holds relational patterns, language model holds, validate our previous, previous hypothesis

备注： 18 pages, 7 figures, 2 tables. Reproduction package (neuron sets, texts, answers, code) in the ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We validate our previous hypothesis that a language model holds relational patterns apart from the subject domains that instantiate them (Nordfors, 2026). These archetypal contexts are patterns of relations that people recognize across many fields, such as the tipping point or winner takes all, held once and applied across domains. We show this inside the model, Qwen3.8-27B. One set of neurons carries the archetype, say the tipping point; another carries the topic, say politics, ecology or cell biology; a third carries the language, such as English, Spanish or Chinese. Silence the tipping point's neurons, and the model no longer identifies the pattern but still knows what the text is about. Silence the topic's, and it loses the topic but still names the pattern. In the neuron maps the layers organize themselves into a sandwich: the outer layers carry languages, the layers inside them topics, the layers at the heart archetypes. The sandwich emerges spontaneously during pretraining and appears in every model we mapped. Archetypes are the architecture for analogy, and we strongly suggest that this structure of archetypes and topics is the mechanism of a language model's intelligence: we see it self-organize as the model is trained. The neurons, the texts and the code are released with this paper as its reproduction package, so that anyone can repeat the test inside the model.

75. 【2611.07232】Recursive Sparse Attention: Million-Token Length Extrapolation at Logarithmic Cost

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07232

作者：Ruitian Wang,Xinyu Yang,Minshen Zhang,Hao Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：target length increases, training typically relies, Recursive Sparse Attention, long documents, dense attention

备注： 24 pages, 6 figures, 17 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:At context lengths of about 1M tokens, dense attention is costly in prefill and decoding, and training typically relies on long documents, which become scarcer as the target length increases. Most sparse attention methods score all preceding blocks or tokens, so their selection cost per query is $O(L)$ at context length $L$; hierarchical selection reduces it to $O(\log L)$, but prior trainable hierarchical methods report accuracy only within their training length. We propose RecSA (Recursive Sparse Attention), which builds a tree over the KV cache in which each node summarizes its subtree with one key and one mean-pooled value. The keys come from one pooler per layer, shared by all levels and distilled from dense attention. Each level above the tokens selects at most $k$ candidates, so each query has $O(\log L)$ selection cost and reads $O(\log L)$ entries; the summaries of unselected candidates share one softmax with the exact tokens. Each entry's RoPE position is its index among the candidates of its level, so at any length every relative distance stays within the range seen during training. Initialized from Qwen3-4B and trained on 0.62B tokens at the original model's 32K training length, RecSA outperforms all baselines on RULER at every length from 131K to 2M; at 1M, it scores 60.5, compared with 40.0 for the best baseline, Dense + DCA. With our optimized kernels, RecSA is faster than dense attention in end-to-end prefill and decoding from 64K to 2M; at 524K on one H200 GPU, it accelerates prefill by 3.2x and decoding by 3.8x.

76. 【2611.07228】Source-balanced Capped Fisher: Bounded Evidence Aggregation for User Inference in Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07228

作者：Yang Liu,Ran Zou,Wanrong Zhu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：User inference, language-model update, source contributed documents, auditor observes, observes only fresh

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:User inference asks whether a source contributed documents to a language-model update, even when the auditor observes only fresh documents from that source. Document-level scores can provide evidence of participation, but combining this evidence is challenging: documents differ in baseline predictability and in how strongly they reflect information learned from other documents of the same source. We propose source-balanced capped Fisher (SCF), an aggregation method that uses known nonparticipating sources to assess how unusual each document score is. It transforms the resulting reference-tail frequencies into negative-log evidence, caps individual contributions, and averages them within each source. This limits the influence of isolated extreme scores while retaining evidence across multiple documents. We complete the inference procedure with a decision rule that tests source participation using a p-value calibrated against a separate set of known nonparticipating sources. The rule provides finite-sample Type-I error control under source exchangeability. Experiments across four real-world datasets show that SCF achieves the highest AUROC among the evaluated aggregation methods in four domains. With the calibration-based decision rule, SCF delivers consistently competitive detection power across the tested settings, achieving the highest power in many cases, while empirical false-positive rates remain generally close to or below the nominal levels.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Adolescents are increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, advice, and companionship, raising safety concerns. While recent evaluations have started to examine safety across multiple conversational turns, few compare how slight changes in a teen's responses can affect a chatbot's safety in the same scenario. We conduct a clinician-informed evaluation of chatbot safety in simulated conversations with adolescents aged 13-17, examining how safety failures vary with adolescents' responses and teen-aware system prompting. We evaluated 32,555 simulated conversations across 18 chatbot APIs and two system-prompt conditions using 22 safety criteria grounded in previously identified harms. Conversations spanned 983 variants of 106 seed scenarios covering urgent safety issues, health advice, and identity and relationships. The overall criterion failure rate was 43.3% without the teen-aware prompt and 24.9% with it. The teen-aware prompt reduced failures, but it didn't eliminate them. 78. 【2611.07216】Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216

作者：Hong kyu Lee,Kai Shu,Li Xiong

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large vision-language models, Large vision-language, isolation become harmful, compositional, Large

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are vulnerable to compositional harm: an image and a prompt that are benign in isolation become harmful through the model's cross-modal reasoning. Recent alignment methods report strong scores on compositional safety benchmarks, but an LLM judge only measures observed behavior and cannot reveal how robustly safety is integrated into the model. We argue that un-alignment provides a complementary audit of alignment robustness. We first show that minimal supervised fine-tuning reaches an LLM-judge score comparable to a multi-stage reinforcement-learning pipeline, so similar benchmark scores cannot establish robustness by themselves. We then show that recognition and refusal of compositional harm are decoupled: nine standard direction-finding methods detect compositional harm with up to AUC=0.99, yet ablating the recovered directions either leaves refusal intact or destroys model coherence. We therefore propose CMaG, a training-free un-alignment method for compositional safety. CMaG measures refusal intention with a refusal-versus-compliance log-probability margin, takes its gradient with respect to mid-layer activations, and contrasts it against partially benign pairs to isolate the cross-modal interaction term. A single backward pass over 80 examples yields a direction along which steering monotonically converts refusal into compliance. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.5-7B, CMaG reduces compositional safety scores by up to 65 points while over 98% of outputs remain coherent, outperforming gradient-based baselines; the direction also transfers to text-explicit harm and un-aligns models hardened by defensive training. Compositional safety can thus appear behaviorally strong while remaining fragile to targeted internal intervention. Warning: this paper contains unsafe model outputs, including references to self-harm.

83. 【2611.07111】Consistent yet Contextual: Learning Author-Level Mixtures of Style for Authorship Attribution

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Approaches to authorship verification or attribution typically model the style of a document, asking whether two documents were likely written by the same author. While recent approaches have been performant, such approaches largely ignore modeling the authors themselves and how varied their style is. Here, we introduce multiple alternatives for modeling the author and demonstrate that allowing an author's representation to capture multiple consistent but distinct writing styles yields significant performance improvements. 84. 【2611.07072】Automated Feedback to Peer Tutors Increases Their Talk Moves and Learner Reasoning but Not the Balance of Talk

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07072

作者：Dorottya Demszky,Yann Hicke,Joy Yun,Mariah Olson,Max Karpawich,Anees Iqbal,Matt Wu,Jimmy Huang,Akshay Ravikumar,Drew Bent

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Emerging Technologies (cs.ET); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：online teaching contexts, delivering similar feedback, classroom discourse improves, discourse improves instruction, recent work suggests

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Automated feedback on classroom discourse improves instruction in professional and online teaching contexts, and recent work suggests that delivering similar feedback to learners may strengthen these effects by sharing responsibility for participation. Whether this holds in small-group volunteer tutoring has not been tested. We present results from a randomized controlled trial across two four-week SAT mathematics bootcamps on the this http URL platform, involving 1,030 volunteer tutors, 7,443 learners and 6,508 recorded sessions. Tutors were randomly assigned to receive no feedback, automated feedback on their own discourse, or that feedback together with participation feedback delivered to their learners under one of two motivational framings. Feedback to tutors increased eliciting of learner ideas, uptake of learner contributions, and learner reasoning by 8-15%, and the pattern replicated across both bootcamps. However, tutor talk time, which averaged 91.5% in the control group, did not change in any condition, and learner-facing feedback did not further change discourse. Interviews with 13 learners suggest why the learner-facing feedback had little effect: it reported a group-level rather than a learner-level statistic, some learners preferred to listen, and learner-to-learner interaction was rare in these sessions. Automated feedback can change how tutors invite learner thinking, but shifting how much learners talk likely requires changes beyond feedback.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Once an agent is live, teams want to know whether it is doing its job, not just whether it throws errors. Yet most evaluation is one-off: a benchmark run, a look at traces, a single score. Its conclusions neither check themselves nor reach the agent. We propose closed-loop evaluation, an evaluation method for deployed agents. It models evaluation as performance estimation under biased raters and censored outcomes, and it requires every level's conclusion to trace back to atomic data and to be computed by versioned rules. The method covers four levels of analysis (span, trace, conversation and engagement), measures along a spine of Execution, Substance, Trajectory and Results, treats Conduct as a constraint throughout, and uses Provenance and Adjudication components to certify the evidence and the evaluators. Evaluator agents generated by an Agent Factory carry these components, each with a domain knowledge pack and an adapter harness. Two loops close the evaluation: an inner loop audits the evidence, rules and metrics themselves, and an outer loop uses per-component regression checks to drive agent improvement. We also give a pipeline that runs this process automatically. Using the business-analysis agent SharpInsight as a case and porting the method to public conversation data, we show how problems differ across levels and how evidence-and-rule judgment is more reliable than holistic scoring (ICC 0.86 vs. 0.81; kappa 0.81 vs. 0.63).

88. 【2611.07025】LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025

作者：Teresa Delgado,Hugo Hazard,Martin Benfeghoul,Jun Wang,Zafeirios Fountas

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Autoregressive decoding relies, memory footprint grows, footprint grows linearly, caps batch size, Autoregressive decoding

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\times$ compression at $94.5\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

89. 【2611.06977】BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977

作者：Xiuyuan Qi,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Conde,Shanshan Liu

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Reproducible LLM inference, LLM inference requires, Reproducible LLM, LLM inference, inference requires

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

Abstract:Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-model Transfer (PACT), a form of latent communication between language models, combines a larger model's prompt representations with a smaller model's efficient generation, offering a promising way to balance capability and inference cost. However, existing systems provide limited support for independently evaluating large-small model collaboration, and improved task performance alone does not establish whether the receiver benefits from the sender's information. We introduce PLatour, an open-source PACT framework that connects frozen models through lightweight, zero-initialized linear bridges trained directly with task rewards and supports hybrid-attention architectures. 91. 【2611.06969】Interactive Backdoor Attacks in LLM-Based Proactive Dialogue Systems

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06969

作者：Zhiyang Xia,Yuming Shang,Xi Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：systems provide goal-directed, provide goal-directed support, LLM-based proactive dialogue, proactive dialogue systems, dialogue systems provide

备注： 26 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LLM-based proactive dialogue systems provide goal-directed support through sustained interaction. Fine-tuning their underlying models on externally supplied dialogue data introduces backdoor risks. Existing LLM backdoor studies primarily focus on eliciting an attacker-specified response upon trigger activation. We present an interactive backdoor attack framework that uses training-data poisoning to steer post-trigger, multi-turn interactions toward an attacker-specified objective. Poisoned fine-tuning dialogues implant a trigger-conditioned guidance-policy bias that ordinary user utterances can activate, without deployment-time prompt modification or further attacker intervention. Specifically, we instantiate three complementary attacks under progressively expanded post-trigger interaction settings: (1) Direct Attack, which targets an immediate response deviation upon trigger activation, retaining the conventional trigger--response setting; (2) Constrained Progressive Attack, which progressively advances the attack objective along a predefined multi-turn trajectory; and (3) Unconstrained Progressive Attack, which extends target-directed steering to open-ended interactions with varied user continuations. Using psychological counseling as a representative setting, experiments on two LLM backbones and two dialogue datasets show that all three attacks effectively achieve their objectives under their respective interaction settings while largely preserving normal utility. Separate defense evaluations show that the tested training-data filters retain nearly all poisoned dialogues, while three post-admission baselines leave substantial residual attack-specific success.

92. 【2611.06956】Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956

作者：Tianming Sha

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：LLM systems explore, Multi-agent LLM systems, LLM systems, single-agent systems append, isolated context windows

备注： 19 pages, 12 figures, 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL). We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read_thread, wait_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\times$ ($57.9\% \to 2.7\%$) while retaining $92.2\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards. Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\times$.

93. 【2608.11676】XBridge: Entity-Grounded Latent Bridge for Heterogeneous LLM Communication

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11676

作者：Wooseong Yang,Wei-Chieh Huang,Weizhi Zhang,Yu Wang,Philip S. Yu,Junhyun Lee

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Heterogeneous multi-agent LLM, multi-agent LLM systems, redundant reasoning patterns, reducing redundant reasoning, Heterogeneous multi-agent

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 18 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Heterogeneous multi-agent LLM systems, where agents are powered by different model families, can outperform homogeneous configurations by reducing redundant reasoning patterns. Yet existing communication protocols either operate through text, discarding the sender's internal representations, or require architectural homogeneity for latent-level transfer. We identify the entity grounding problem in cross-architecture communication: cross-attention bridges that transfer continuous representations across different LLM families suffer from rare-token compression collapse, where entity identity is lost in the continuous bottleneck (bridge-only F1 ~30%). We propose XBRIDGE, a decode-free communication protocol that addresses this through two mechanisms. Lexical Anchor Mapping (LAM) maps the sender's original context tokens to the receiver's vocabulary, providing discrete entity anchors. A Latent Enrichment Bridge (LEB) lets the receiver query the sender's hidden states for contextual enrichment. The entity anchors ground the bridge's contextual signals to specific entities through the receiver's own self-attention. Across three model families (Llama, Qwen, and Mistral), seven benchmarks, and both communication directions, XBRIDGE outperforms text-based communication on all seven tasks for each model pair while achieving 11x lower latency, and in a same-architecture setting it also exceeds a KV-sharing baseline on six of seven tasks. LEB requires only 264M trainable parameters (3.8% of the receiver), is trained on a small balanced sample set, and adds negligible inference overhead.

94. 【2611.07845】Masked diffusion as random sequential adsorption: an exactly solvable model for parallel decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07845

作者：Adrian Baule

类目：atistical Mechanics (cond-mat.stat-mech); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Masked diffusion models, diffusion models generate, models generate text, Masked diffusion, stochastic masking process

备注： 14 pages (incl. appendices), 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Masked diffusion models generate text by reversing a stochastic masking process. Unlike autoregressive LLMs, which generate text one token at a time, they can reveal many tokens in parallel, at the price of an error, since tokens revealed together are drawn independently. We show that this trade-off maps onto the statistical physics of driven many-particle systems. (i) For any data, the error of parallel generation is the entropy production of the generation process relative to the reversed masking process. (ii) For Markov data, generation is a random sequential adsorption process with labelled gaps, which we solve exactly for any number of steps. (iii) For many steps, the error becomes the dissipation of a slowly driven system: the unmasking schedule plays the role of the driving protocol, the number of steps that of its duration, and the error is set by a thermodynamic metric that we obtain in closed form from the mutual information between tokens. Optimal schedules are geodesics of this metric. The exact solution shows how correlations in the text shape both the error and the quality of the output. The longer the correlations, the more the error concentrates at the start of generation. Schedules that start with small steps then gain more, both in path error and in perplexity, and need fewer steps to reach their asymptotic behaviour.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08325

Abstract:Retrieved web text can carry instructions aimed at the language model, or synthetic pages written to mislead. We test whether released open-weight decision models, which answer typed questions about a text without generating tokens, can screen retrieved passages against both threats. On a benchmark of 1,000 questions over forum-style web pages with hidden injected instructions, a Kev-4B gate reads each passage's HTML. Attack success, measured automatically, falls from 27.9% with no defence to 13.6% with the published mitigations (sanitisation, normalisation and citation prompting) and to 5.7% with the gate added. Filtering only the top ten leaves the generator too few pages to answer. Large LLMs used as judges rank attacks better (AUROC 0.99 against 0.94) but cost ten times more. Gates catch more attacks when they read the page's HTML than when they read its plain text. Every model is open-weight.

6. 【2611.07928】ZeroRAG: Adaptive Semantic Peer Routing for Decentralized Retrieval-Augmented Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07928

作者：Petru Neague,Quinten Stokkink,Martijn de Vos,Rafael Pires,Mathis Randl,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Johan Pouwelse

类目：Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing (cs.DC); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Networking and Internet Architecture (cs.NI)

关键词：large language model, Retrieval-augmented generation, language model, outputs by retrieving, standard approach

备注： 14 pages, 8 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a standard approach to enhance the reliability of large language model (LLM) outputs by retrieving documents relevant to a query and supplying them in the LLM prompt. Today's RAG systems collect, index, and serve these documents from centralized stores. In practice, however, documents are owned by organizations, devices, and individuals who cannot or will not surrender custody of them to a central party, e.g., because of privacy, copyright or regulatory compliance. Existing modern decentralized retrieval techniques either reintroduce a coordinating authority, or rely on a global data structure that requires continuous network-wide coordination (e.g., DHTs). We present ZeroRAG, a decentralized routing layer for document retrieval in RAG that performs semantic query routing across an overlay of peers with- out global data structures or centralized orchestration. Documents remain with the peers that own them, and each peer keeps only lightweight local state: a bounded set of neighbor advertisements and a cache of recently routed queries. Using these, ZeroRAG steers each query through a best-first walk that progressively rewires the overlay toward semantically relevant neighbors, so recall improves over time as the network self-organizes. We evaluate ZeroRAG using three real-world workloads and two decentralized baselines. We find that ZeroRAG outperforms baselines in terms of recall and required network messages, approaches the recall and LLM answer accuracy of a centralized document store, self-heals after catastrophic peer failures, and scales to 100 000 peers.

8. 【2611.07859】CORE: Coarse-to-Fine Search with Context Management for Deep Research

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07859

作者：Hoang Tran Vuong,Ngoc Quan Pham,Hung Bui,Fatih Porikli,Dat Quoc Nguyen

关键词：Deep research agents, Deep research, answer complex questions, complex questions, Deep

备注： 24 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deep research agents answer complex questions through repeated search. They rely on a harness that runs their tool calls and returns evidence to their context. This creates a trade-off: Search works best over long passages, which give enough context for reliable semantic matching. But the returned evidence should be short, because every observation stays in the agent's context for the rest of the trajectory. We introduce CORE, a training-free harness that combines coarse-to-fine search with context management. CORE retrieves passages with a dense retriever. It then reuses the token-level similarities from a late-interaction reranker to return only the most relevant snippet from each passage. Context management lets the agent expand the context around sources it has already retrieved and suppresses repeated snippets. In controlled closed-search comparisons with the Tongyi DeepResearch and OpenResearcher harnesses, CORE achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and evidence recall across three agent models and three benchmarks (BrowseComp-Plus, BrowseComp-Wikipedia and BrowseComp-Plus-CM), on corpora containing up to 553M documents.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07851

作者：DaEun Choi,Daniel Lee

类目：Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：recommender systems treat, systems treat user, infer and optimize, depending on context, treat user preferences

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Most recommender systems treat user preferences as a single signal to infer and optimize, yet human preferences are a mix of a stable preference and situational preferences that turn on and off depending on context. Through Tasteply, we externalize this preference structure as manipulable spatial objects. Tasteply represents each situational preference as an attractor and the stable preference as an underlying global field. Users can adjust their feed by moving around the space and adjusting the strength of the global field, turning the preference structure that remains latent within the model into something they can see and act on directly to steer the recommendation algorithm. Through an exploratory study (N=5), we found that this spatial control was effective, along with its limits and future direction as a means of manipulating preference-based recommendations.

10. 【2611.07631】MEMOIR: A Training-Free Memory Lifecycle for Long-Horizon LLM Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07631

作者：Mayank Anand,Subham Raj,Aman Vaibhav Jha,Sriparna Saha

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Long-horizon LLM agents, Long-horizon LLM, LLM agents require, preserve useful information, information across sessions

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that preserve useful information across sessions and retrieve it when needed. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: memories are written prospectively with static importance scores that are never revised after use. This creates two coupled failure modes: consolidation can silently destroy evidence that future queries require, while retrieval continues to favor stale memories. We propose MEMOIR (Memory Evidence via Merge-certified, Online Importance Revision), a training-free memory lifecycle that unifies judge-gated consolidation with retrospective attribution. Before compressing memory, screening gates test whether answer-relevant evidence is preserved. After each query, attribution estimates which retrieved memories supported the answer and updates their retrieval priority. A UCB-style exploration bonus additionally encourages retrieval of under-exposed memories, reducing the risk that frequently retrieved memories crowd out useful but less-seen evidence. On 1,905 deduplicated LoCoMo QA pairs, the full retrieval stack improves evidence recall@5 by 11.92 percentage points over bare cosine, with confidence-gated feedback adding 1.50 points overall without reference answers or reranker training; a deployment-unavailable oracle adds 1.84 points overall and 8.05 points on multi-hop. Judge-gated merge changes the recall of text-based evidence from 58.82% to 59.08%, reduces the active store by 1.42%, and incurs 0 observed losses in 100 independently audited merges, with a one-sided 95% upper loss-rate bound of 2.95%. Together, these results outline a practical deployment path: no additional LLM call on the online write path, with merge screening confined to periodic offline consolidation. The lifecycle reduces to one principle: attribute usefulness after use, and screen for preservation before compression.

11. 【2611.07591】Beyond Independent Predictions: Learning the Browsing Journey for Sequence-Level Re-ranking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07591

作者：Jiayi Zhang,Jingxin Hai,Zhenjia Ma,Hongbin Pei,Dagui Chen,Han Zhu,Jian Xu

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Re-ranking aims, maximizes sequence-level utility, aims to present, ordered list, list that maximizes

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Re-ranking aims to present users with an ordered list that maximizes sequence-level utility. Industrial deployments widely adopt the Generator--Evaluator framework, where the generator proposes candidate sequences, and the evaluator scores them to select the best one for display. The fidelity of the evaluator's utility estimates thus directly governs recommendation quality. However, existing evaluators predominantly follow the \textbf{Independent Behavior Modeling (IBM)} paradigm, which simplifies the continuous user \emph{browsing journey} into independent per-item predictions (e.g., CTR). This oversimplification ignores two physical realities: (i) a click can occur only after the item has been reached and impressed, and (ii) items compete for finite user attention. As a result, IBM produces systematically biased evaluations and suboptimal sequence selection. To address this, we propose a \textbf{Joint Behavior Modeling (JBM)} perspective that explicitly models the \emph{display} $\rightarrow$ \emph{impression} $\rightarrow$ \emph{click} process to capture causal and competitive dependencies within a sequence. Building on JBM, we develop the \textbf{Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)}, a plug-and-play framework that (i) predicts a browsing-depth distribution to estimate per-position impression probabilities, (ii) leverages predicted impressions to suppress spurious context from unexposed items during click modeling, and (iii) introduces a click distribution matching objective to capture intra-sequence dependencies. Extensive offline experiments across diverse backbones, together with a 14-day online A/B test in the Taobao App (+0.70\% clicks, +3.67\% revenue) and full-traffic deployment, validate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach.

12. 【2611.07588】What Gets Hacked in a Collaborative Reward for LLM Recommenders: The Part That Does Not Depend on the User

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07588

作者：Zhuangsheng Li,Liang Dong,Yu Wu,Dailin Li,Yu Xia,Yunhan C. Zhang,Rui Cai,Yi Wu,Julian McAuley

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：large language model, scores provide graded, training large language, provide graded, recommenders through reinforcement

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Collaborative-filtering (CF) scores provide graded, personalized rewards for training large language model (LLM) recommenders through reinforcement learning. However, these rewards can cause exposure collapse: even though the CF model itself produces diverse top recommendations across users, an LLM optimized with its rewards concentrates on fewer items. We decompose each score into an item-mean component, the item's average CF score across user histories, and a residual component, the current user's score deviation from the average. Through controlled interventions, we identify the item-mean component as a contributor to exposure collapse. Accordingly, we propose two reward corrections: per-item centering removes each item's estimated mean score, while per-item standardization further rescales the residual using its estimated standard deviation. Experiments on two datasets with two identifier schemes show that both corrections substantially improve catalog coverage and exposure evenness. Compared with training on the uncorrected CF reward, our corrections increase the number of distinct items ranked first across users by up to $9.8\times$, while maintaining comparable ranking accuracy. Code is available at this https URL.

13. 【2611.07523】OptST: Optimizing Semantic Tokenization for LLM-Enhanced Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07523

作者：Hanchong Chen,Jie Liu,Fuyuan Lyu,Dugang Liu,Lingjie Li,Xiuqiang He,Xing Tang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：enrich the identifiers, Semantic tokenization, Semantic, structured fields, Optimizing Semantic Tokenization

备注： Accepted to Findings of WSDM 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Semantic information can enrich the identifiers and structured fields used by recommender systems. Semantic tokenization provides a structured representation of this information by converting LLM-derived user profiles into discrete tokens. In the common two-stage pipeline, the tokenizer is trained before the recommender. Its token assignments are then treated as fixed input features during recommendation training. As a result, the downstream recommender can learn how to use the assigned indices, but it cannot revise the semantic partition that produced them. Making this partition task-aware is difficult because nearest-code assignment is discrete, and unconstrained adaptation may also distort the geometry learned during pretraining. Moreover, a fixed token depth exposes the same semantic resolution to every interaction. We thus propose OptST (Optimizing Semantic Tokenization), which makes a pretrained tokenizer responsive to downstream recommendation. To revise token assignments without directly updating the pretrained encoder or base codebooks, OptST learns a low-rank deformation whose drift is limited by an anchor regularizer. Recommendation supervision reaches this transformation through a straight-through estimator, while prediction retains hard nearest-code assignments. Beyond adapting the assignments themselves, OptST selects an interaction-conditioned prefix to control how much residual detail is exposed to the recommender. Experiments on four public datasets show that OptST achieves the highest AUC among conventional and LLM-enhanced baselines. Its improvements also persist across different recommendation backbones. These results establish task-aware semantic tokenization as an effective interface between LLM-derived profiles and downstream recommendation models.

14. 【2611.07486】MEvince: Knowledge-Constrained LLM Reasoning for Technique Identification from Malware Reports

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07486

作者：Hongyu She,Yiling He,Mengda Xie,Yiping Liu,Xingzhi Qian,Zhan Qin,Lorenzo Cavallaro

类目：Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Mapping behavioral intelligence, identification remains challenging, reliable identification remains, supports threat analysis, Mapping behavioral

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Mapping behavioral intelligence in malware analysis reports to ATTCK techniques supports threat analysis and defense, but reliable identification remains challenging. Supporting evidence is often scattered across long, heterogeneous reports, while closely related techniques share behavioral cues and differ only in subtle but decisive details. While large language models (LLMs) can use external knowledge to interpret reports, they may assign techniques based on plausible associations rather than sufficient evidence. We present MEvince, a knowledge-constrained LLM reasoning framework for report-level malware technique identification. Its core is a shared behavioral representation connecting report observations to technique definitions. This representation is built through agentic mining of technique documentation and supports two online stages: 1) primitive-guided extraction uses behavioral primitives to structure LLM-extracted observations, link them to source passages, and group dispersed evidence; 2) evidence-aligned retrieval matches this evidence to technique profiles through the same concepts, retaining likely candidates and confusable alternatives for LLM comparison of distinguishing behaviors. Controls at both stages validate evidence references and check technique-specific requirements. The representation also supports adaptation without parameter updates, where offline agentic audits diagnose concept weaknesses and refine extraction or retrieval policies. We evaluate MEvince against 11 baselines spanning four method families using a dataset of 610 public malware reports referenced by MITRE ATTCK. MEvince improves exact-ID F1 over the strongest baseline by 17.4%, and its concept-guided retrieval increases gold-label candidate coverage from 31.6% to 47.9%. With a small adaptation set, offline refinement yields further gains and outperforms baselines that update model parameters.

15. 【2611.07425】BRIDGE: Bridging the Semantic Quantization Gap for Generative Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07425

作者：Yatong Xiao,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Generative recommendation retrieves, recommendation retrieves items, Generative recommendation, generating Semantic IDs, RQ-VAE quantizes

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative recommendation retrieves items by autoregressively generating Semantic IDs(SIDs), where an RQ-VAE quantizes each item into a hierarchical SID sequence that the decoder generates level by level. We identify an overlooked bottleneck in this paradigm: the Semantic Quantization Gap(SQG). Quantization derives SIDs from rich continuous signals, including codebook centroids, local neighborhood geometry, and level-wise entropy. However, the decoder receives only discrete identifiers and loses the semantic structure that shaped them. This mismatch manifests in three ways: a grounding gap, where the decoder lacks semantic anchors when target-side information is limited; a neighborhood gap, where token-level supervision discards inter-item similarity and exacerbates exposure bias; and a difficulty gap, where computation is allocated uniformly despite generation difficulty diminishing with depth. To bridge the SQG, we propose BRIDGE. Codebook-grounded latent reasoning injects frozen quantizer centroids as semantic anchors before SID generation. Privileged EMA self-distillation transfers neighborhood-aware knowledge from a teacher to an on-policy student, preserving inter-item geometry while mitigating exposure bias. Effort-aware recurrence allocates more computation to the challenging early levels and progressively reduces it with depth. Across three Amazon benchmarks, BRIDGE improves Recall@10 and NDCG@10 by 10.9% and 9.7% on average over the strongest baseline (up to 12.6% and 10.7%). In a one-week online A/B experiment on a large-scale e-commerce advertising platform with 5% traffic, BRIDGE improves eCPM by 0.65%.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07393

作者：Vihan Lakshman,Peter Baile Chen,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Recent work, transformer-based language model, language model agents, space via key-value, work in machine

备注： 5 pages. Third KEIR Workshop @ CIKM 2026. Work in progress

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent work in machine learning has demonstrated that transformer-based language model agents can communicate state not only through text, but also directly in latent space via key-value (KV) caches. In this paper, we argue that such cache-to-cache translation can provide utility beyond the original motivating application of real-time agent communication and can instead be utilized for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Building on the Cache-to-Cache (C2C) fusion framework, our proposed system CacheGraft encodes each corpus passage offline into a fused KV cache representation between a larger sharer model (4B parameters in our experiments) and a smaller receiver model (0.6B parameters) such that this cache can be injected into the receiver at query time with no need for the larger model. Notably, these gains are zero-shot with respect to the fuser, which was never trained on retrieval data. These initial results suggest that latent-space document enrichment may be a promising avenue for enhancing the performance of RAG systems, particularly in compute-constrained settings where it is only feasible to deploy a small model at inference time.

17. 【2611.07298】Reducing Representational Interference in Retrieval Embeddings with Trainable Prefix Tokens

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07298

作者：Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：compress variable-length text, entangle semantic content, fixed-size vector representations, models compress variable-length, Embedding models compress

备注： CIKM 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Embedding models compress variable-length text into fixed-size vector representations, but this compression can entangle semantic content with residual token-level information, such as artifacts arising from attention sink behavior. We propose a simple method that prepends trainable [DUMP] tokens to the input during embedding model training. The method keeps the model architecture, pooling strategy, and training loss unchanged, while optimizing the added tokens through the standard contrastive retrieval objective. By introducing trainable prefix positions that interact with regular tokens during encoding, [DUMP] tokens help reduce the influence of residual or uninformative token-level signals on the final retrieval representation. Experiments on standard retrieval and long-context benchmarks show that our method consistently improves over finetuned baselines across encoder and decoder embedding models. Further analysis shows that [DUMP] tokens alter token-level activation and attention patterns associated with low-information tokens. These results suggest that trainable prefix tokens provide a lightweight way to improve retrieval representations.

Abstract:Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. 2. 【2611.08485】Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485

作者：David Yan,Le Xue,Forrest Huang,Qiuyi Zhang,Dustin Tran,Andrew M. Dai,Yinfei Yang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Grounded reasoning, solving complex visual, reasoning, Grounded, relevant locations

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://elorian.ai/blog/why-grounding)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

4. 【2611.08449】VFlash: Fast, Accurate Visual Planning via Flash Primitive Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08449

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Models for physical AI tasks, such as robotics and autonomous driving, need not reason solely in language. They can use visual plans--points, bounding boxes, and trajectories--to represent spatial goals and anticipate motion. However, autoregressive vision-language models generate these plans one coordinate token at a time, making richer predictions increasingly costly. We introduce VFlash, a method for fast, accurate visual planning through Flash Primitive Decoding. VFlash exploits the known structure of visual primitives to predict their coordinates jointly, using a lightweight drafter that generates an entire primitive in one forward pass. The layout adapts to the primitive's type, dimensionality, and length, supporting compact boxes and long, variable-length trajectories. Crucially, spatial accuracy need not require reproducing an autoregressive model's exact token sequence. VFlash therefore trains on annotated primitives and directly accepts structurally valid predictions without target-token agreement. Across the evaluated 2D and 3D robot trajectory tasks, VFlash consistently reduces prediction errors relative to autoregressive decoding. Preliminary single-rollout measurements indicate approximately 25x faster generation for 3D point motion forecasting. A world action model conditioned on VFlash plans achieves 70.2% average success across four MolmoSpaces task suites, compared with 62.6% for the baseline.

6. 【2611.08436】SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://sm4rt-eailab.github.io/)

Abstract:We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. 7. 【2611.08413】3R: Training-Free Two-Stage Token Refinement Towards Efficient and Robust VLA Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08413

作者：Mohammed Hassan,Zhenyang Chen,Zheng Wang,Zhixin Zhu,Tianlong Chen,Yingyan(Celine)Lin,Chaojian Li

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：models enable, robot control, increasingly expensive, visual tokens, tokens

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable end-to-end robot control, but inference is increasingly expensive. Each decision processes hundreds of visual tokens, often across multiple cameras, leading to substantial computation while allowing irrelevant background content to distract action prediction under distribution shift. We argue that these issues stem from two complementary forms of redundancy: many visual tokens are spatially irrelevant, while attention over the remaining tokens is often misallocated. Based on this observation, we propose T3R, a Training-Free Two-Stage Token Refinement framework. In the first stage, segmentation-guided token pruning removes irrelevant visual patches to reduce computation and eliminate distracting context. In the second stage, saliency-guided attention biasing reallocates attention toward action-critical visual regions and language instructions within the retained tokens. Together, pruning produces a cleaner candidate set, while attention biasing further emphasizes the most decision-relevant evidence, yielding a better accuracy-efficiency frontier than either stage alone. On LIBERO-Spatial, T3R removes 85% of scene-camera visual tokens while essentially matching the unpruned success rate, and improves success by up to 7 percentage points under out-of-distribution object-placement perturbations. The timing comparison attributes 7.8 ms per inference call to pruning and attention-bias application. As input resolution increases, LLM-forward profiling shows up to a 3.3x speedup over the unpruned baseline. Evaluations across LIBERO, SIMPLER, and RoboTwin with three VLA backbones show the strongest accuracy-efficiency benefits in multi-camera settings. These results demonstrate that training-free token refinement is a practical approach to efficient and robust VLA inference.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08407

Abstract:Humanoid robots are typically trained as solitary agents, leaving them unable to coordinate physically with human partners who naturally anticipate and adjust to one another. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08372

作者：Sateesh Kumar,Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Skand Peri,Georgios Pavlakos,Roberto Martín-Martín

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/momasplat)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visuomotor policies trained through imitation learning are highly sensitive to changes in camera viewpoint. This is particularly problematic for mobile manipulation (MoMa), where robots rarely execute manipulation from the exact same base pose used during demonstration collection due to navigation uncertainty, localization drift, and environmental constraints. Recent approaches use novel-view synthesis (NVS) to augment demonstrations with unseen viewpoints, but they assume static scenes and therefore fail to re-render the manipulated object during the post-contact interaction phase, leaving the most critical parts of the demonstration unaugmented. We present MOMASPLAT, a novel-view demonstration augmentation framework for mobile manipulation that synthesizes full manipulation trajectories from unseen robot base poses, including dynamic object interactions. Our key insight is that standard MoMa data collection naturally provides the multiview observations needed to reconstruct both the static scene and the manipulated object in 3D. MOMASPLAT combines 3D Gaussian Splatting, feed-forward 3D foundation models, and kinematics-based action retargeting to separately reconstruct the scene and manipulated object, render them from novel viewpoints, and generate geometrically consistent synthetic demonstrations paired with transformed actions. We evaluate MOMASPLAT on five real-world mobile manipulation tasks involving both rigid and articulated objects across three scenes, and validate the fidelity of its synthesized demonstrations against ground truth in simulation. MOMASPLAT substantially improves robustness to unseen base poses, more than doubling the average success rate of the strongest baseline and maintaining non-trivial success at large base displacements where all baselines fail, while reducing the need for largescale real-world data collection.

点击查看摘要

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342

作者：Adnan Armouti,Guanyue Qian,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Signal Processing (eess.SP)

关键词：Millimeter-wave radar resolves, Millimeter-wave radar, enabling gait, vital-sign sensing, resolves the micrometer

备注： Under Review

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Hand avatar reconstruction typically relies on multi-view capture or per-subject optimization, limiting its scalability. We introduce InstantHand, a feed-forward framework that constructs an animatable 3D Gaussian hand avatar from a single image using off-the-shelf MANO, camera, and mask estimates, without test-time optimization. Image-averaged losses provide insufficient supervision for alpha leakage, which thickens thin finger contours. To address this issue, we propose a Soft Alpha Band regularizer that concentrates supervision on silhouette boundaries. Furthermore, colors regressed from image features can drift toward a learned prior even in observed regions. Our Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer preserves observed appearance by transferring source colors according to each Gaussian's rendering contribution, while completing unobserved regions using the learned prior conditioned on global source identity. InstantHand constructs an avatar in 2.84 seconds and renders at 161 FPS, matching per-subject-optimized baselines on InterHand2.6M and remaining competitive in zero-shot evaluation on HanCo.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: this https URL

23. 【2611.08240】FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240

作者：Haitao Lin,Minsi Ren,Yang Li,Xingtong Ge,Zheyuan Hu,Peiyan Hu,Tailin Wu

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：rollouts typically dominate, Reinforcement learning, standard post-training method, many-step sampler, standard post-training

备注： 86pages, 12 figures, technical report

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation. The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

24. 【2611.08236】Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236

作者：Carlos Boned Riera,David Romero Sanchez,Oriol Ramos Terrades

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Vision Transformers, token count, carries a quadratic, Self-attention carries, axis-factorized Gaussian kernels

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\% higher throughput and over 60\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.
