备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\vec{\tau}$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods. For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at this https URL.

26. 【2611.08185】Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185

作者：Gizem Mert,Szymon Płotka,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Ewa Szczurek

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：diagnostic cues concentrated, specific anatomical regions, levels of organization, reports are inherently, diagnostic cues

备注： Accepted at WACV 2027. Copyright 2027 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at this http URL.

27. 【2611.08157】Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157

作者：Jinen Setpal,Chaoyue Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Image classification models, Image classification, test time, encounter objects, objects in diverse

备注： accepted to NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions. Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

28. 【2611.08156】La-JEPA: Compact Robot Policies with Latent World Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08156

作者：Mateo Clémente,George Z. Xue,Amin Abyaneh,Liheng Ma,Rui Heng Yang,Tongtong Cao,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：World-action models adapt, cost to large, models adapt pretrained, pretrained vision-language backbones, large transformers designed

备注： 30 pages, 6 figures. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/mt-clemente/lajepa)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World-action models adapt pretrained video generators, and vision-language-action models build on pretrained vision-language backbones; both tie policy architecture and inference cost to large transformers designed for generation rather than control. It remains unclear how much these generation-oriented choices affect control. In this work, we introduce Latent Action JEPA (La-JEPA), a compact world-action model whose policy transformer is trained from scratch in the feature space of a pretrained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. Following Fast-WAM, La-JEPA learns to predict future features jointly with actions during training and generates only actions at inference. Among policies without embodied pretraining, La-JEPA achieves state-of-the-art performance in clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 scenes and on LIBERO-Plus with correct instructions, while using far fewer parameters than the strongest competing policies. It runs substantially faster than Fast-WAM at comparable performance and outperforms the closest JEPA-based methods, LeapBot-WA and JEPA-WAM, on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0 without their pretrained policy backbones. On four real-robot tasks, its average success is comparable to pi0.5 and above non-embodied-pretrained baselines. Through a design study of the choices behind La-JEPA, we show that JEPA features outperform video VAE latents, most clearly out of distribution, and that training-time future prediction choices significantly impact the policy even when future prediction is skipped at inference. We correct the LIBERO-Plus instructions, as the officially released evaluator corrupts them for most variants, and re-evaluate several strong open baselines. Without a generative pretrained policy backbone, La-JEPA is better and faster than far larger policies, suggesting that robot foundation models may benefit from architectures designed for control rather than inherited from generation.

29. 【2611.08154】Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154

作者：Sina Raeisadigh,Henning Müller,Abderrahmane Hedjoudje

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Image and Video Processing (eess.IV)

备注： 5 pages, 2 figures, 1 table

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This study evaluates the added prognostic value of day-1 multimodal MRI for predicting three-month functional outcome after acute ischemic stroke (AIS). Sixty-six AIS patients with ADC, DWI, FLAIR, and clinical data were analyzed. Three-dimensional pretrained ResNet embeddings were extracted from each MRI modality, reduced using fold-isolated principal component analysis, and combined with structured clinical variables and ADC-derived lesion biomarkers. Linear support vector machines were evaluated using eight-fold stratified group cross-validation across MRI modality combinations. Clinical-only modeling achieved strong performance (AUC = $0.899 \pm 0.151$), while MRI-only models showed limited discrimination (AUC $\approx$ $0.67$-$0.73$). Among multimodal configurations, FLAIR-clinical fusion achieved the highest mean cross-validated performance (AUC = $0.948 \pm 0.061$), followed by ADC-clinical fusion (AUC = $0.941 \pm 0.094$). Feature-importance analysis identified age, NIHSS subscores, ADC lesion burden, and learned FLAIR representations as key contributors, while qualitative FLAIR activation maps showed responses over lesion-like regions. These findings suggest that compact day-1 FLAIR and ADC representations provide complementary prognostic information beyond clinical variables and support interpretable, data-efficient outcome prediction in AIS.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148

作者：Ioannis Dravilas,Miruna Stoicescu,Roope Tervo,Carlos Horn,Oriol Hinojo Comellas,Sina Montazeri,Andrea Meraner,Ana Lucic

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08147

作者：Enmin Lin,Jona Beysens

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Hardware Architecture (cs.AR)

备注： Submitted to IEEE ISCAS 2027 (under review). 5 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at this https URL.

32. 【2611.08140】Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140

作者：Tao Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：unsupervised industrial anomaly, industrial anomaly detection, Feature reconstruction, reconstruction is widely, unsupervised industrial

备注： 20 pages including appendix; 4 figures and 21 tables. Preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

34. 【2611.08136】How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136

作者：Hyojun Yi,Geunyoung Kim,Hogeon Seo,Seunghyoung Ryu

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：require learned features, representation-learning problem, strong performance, performance is expected, expected to require

备注： 16 pages, 13 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128

作者：Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Samy Metari,Robert Laganière

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

Abstract:World-action models (WAMs) jointly model future traffic states and driving actions for end-to-end autonomous driving in intelligent transportation systems. Abstract:Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. 42. 【2611.08080】Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080

作者：S-Wei Chen,Siangyu Yang,Yi-Chen Jan,Chun-Yi Lee,Shao-Yuan Lo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Industrial Anomaly Detection, Industrial Anomaly, quality control, long-established problem, problem that aims

备注： Accepted at IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV) 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

44. 【2611.08005】Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005

作者：Songhe Wang,David Miller,Charles A. Kamhoua

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Current language-guided, placement typically predicts, dictated by contact, typically predicts, reality that indoor

备注： 23 pages, 4 figures; includes technical appendices

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07994

作者：Zehao Wang,Si Wu,Guiping Cao,Xin Li,Yaowei Wang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：tiny instances occupy, detection remains challenging, Tiny object detection, object detection remains, remains challenging

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tiny object detection remains challenging because tiny instances occupy only a few pixels, and their fine-grained details are easily lost during feature downsampling. Although the highest-resolution feature preserves rich local details for tiny objects, DETR-like detectors often discard it due to its large computational cost, while directly reintroducing this feature also brings dense background noise. To address this problem, we propose ReDHF, a novel framework that Revives the Discarded High-resolution Feature for tiny object detection. ReDHF has three cooperative components. Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) then guides the enhanced feature to learn more reliable classification and localization priors by applying geometry-adaptive auxiliary supervision. Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) further enables decoder queries to access both low-level details and high-level semantic information, allowing them to aggregate richer cues for tiny objects. Extensive experiments show that ReDHF consistently improves multiple baselines across different DETR variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the evaluated benchmarks. The best ReDHF variants surpass their baselines by +1.4 AP on AI-TOD-v2, +1.6 AP on VisDrone, and +1.5 AP on SODA-D, with especially notable gains on tiny and small objects.

Abstract:Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. 48. 【2611.07955】Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955

作者：Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：avoiding iterative multi-view, iterative multi-view optimization, predict camera poses, camera poses, avoiding iterative

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward 3D models use Transformers to predict camera poses, depth, and structure in one pass, avoiding iterative multi-view optimization. Long sequences and deep attention incur high compute and memory costs, motivating Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) for deployment. Existing PTQ methods mainly suppress channel-wise outliers by smoothing or orthogonal rotation. Geometry Transformers introduce a different challenge: heterogeneous token types share one activation quantization grid. A few special tokens may exert high leverage over global outputs and be updated by only a subset of blocks. Token-blind transforms, which apply the same transformation to every token, reduce overall quantization error but cannot remove differentiated degradation across token types. We propose QMapAnything, a structure-aware PTQ framework. We cast floating-point-preserving rotation and smoothing as a token-blind reparameterization group, and prove that they yield common-mode gains while leaving relative token SNR unchanged. We introduce a statistic $\Gamma$ that combines a special token's quantization disadvantage and output leverage to predict whether differentiated bias correction is needed beyond rotation. Experiments on geometric benchmarks show that W4A8 closely matches full-precision performance. Under W8A8, peak memory falls by $2.2$--$3.1\times$ versus FP32, with up to $3.1\times$ speedup on real hardware. These results provide a principled and efficient approach to deploying feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained edges.

备注： 10 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

Abstract:Large-scale identity-labeled face datasets are essential for training modern face recognition models, yet collecting and annotating such data is costly and raises privacy and ethical concerns. In this paper, we propose IDPrior, a two-stage framework that decouples identity generation from image synthesis. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. 56. 【2611.07902】XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902

作者：Haoyu Han,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki,Jiahui Lei,Heng Yang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO); Optimization and Control (math.OC)

关键词：entails highly nonconvex, reconstruction remains challenging, deformation entails highly, highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate 4D reconstruction remains challenging: jointly estimating camera motion, scene geometry, and non-rigid deformation entails highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality difficult to guarantee. We present XM4D, a framework for accurate and efficient 4D reconstruction built upon certifiably globally optimal geometric optimization. By separating the estimation of camera motion from non-rigid deformation, we formulate both stages as structured quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQP) that admit semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations and verifiable global optimality certificates. For camera motion estimation, we lift static point tracks into 3D using foundational depth prediction models and formulate a scaled bundle adjustment problem that jointly estimates camera poses, depth scales, and static landmarks. By analytically eliminating translations and landmarks, we reduce the problem to a QCQP over scaled rotations. For non-rigid reconstruction, we represent dynamic motion using an embedded deformation graph (EDG) and show that its tracking, rigidity, and temporal regularization objectives similarly reduce to a rotation-only QCQP. Both problems admit SDP relaxations that we solve efficiently with a GPU-accelerated Burer-Monteiro solver based on Riemannian optimization. The recovered geometry further initializes dynamic Gaussian splatting for photometric refinement. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate accurate camera motion estimation on both static and dynamic scenes, efficient optimization that scales to thousands of frames, and state-of-the-art novel-view rendering quality.

57. 【2611.07900】Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900

作者：Takuhiro Kaneko

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Graphics (cs.GR); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：hidden interior structure, robotic manipulation, nondestructive inspection, essential for predictive, interior structure

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027. Project page: [this https URL](https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at this https URL.

Abstract:Panoramic video depth estimation is pivotal for applications such as Virtual Reality and World Models. However, advancements in this field are impeded by two primary obstacles: the scarcity of large-scale training data and the unique spatiotemporal challenges of Equirectangular Projection (ERP), which hinder the direct transfer of perspective models. Abstract:Building a playable world requires more than generating plausible video: it demands responsive control, persistent spatial structure, and coherent interactions with dynamic entities. 链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775

作者：Moritz Grävinghoff,Konstantin Wehmeyer,Aron Asefaw,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：treated as finished, finished artifacts, linear PCA basis, adapters, latent

备注： Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Neural Network Artifacts as a New Data Modality

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. this https URL

72. 【2611.07741】Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741

作者：Qiufu Li,Huibin Xiao,Linlin Shen,Weijia Fan,Xi Jia,Yongdong Huang

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：interrelated fundamental tasks, closely interrelated fundamental, feature learning, Classification, classification accuracy

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $\gamma$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $\gamma$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $\gamma$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $\gamma$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

73. 【2611.07730】Seeing Beyond the Next Step: World-Model-Guided Human-Like Navigation in Multi-Agent Scenes

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07730

作者：Seong-Eun Hong,JuYeong Hwang,Jinhyun Kim,Hanyoung Jang,HyeongYeop Kang

类目：Graphics (cs.GR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：crossing pedestrians, narrow passage, matter of avoiding, locally feasible, feasible yet drift

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. 37 pages, 13 figures, 12 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Selecting a next motion in a populated scene is not just a matter of avoiding what is currently in the way: an action can be locally feasible yet drift onto the road instead of the crosswalk, cut into a stream of crossing pedestrians, or bottleneck a narrow passage. These failure modes are invisible from the current state alone, and acting on them requires reasoning about what each candidate action will cause. World models offer exactly this, namely action-conditioned predictions of the future, but rolling them out at every decision step is a cost deployable navigation systems cannot pay, and the field has largely traded foresight for speed. We argue this is a false dichotomy. The natural place for a world model in human-aware navigation is at training time, as a preference oracle that distills foresight into a fast policy, rather than at inference time, as an online planner. We pair a discrete visual action prior with two task-aligned world models, one for nearby social dynamics and one for future scene semantics, that score candidate actions by their predicted consequences and refine the policy through group-relative preference distillation. At deployment, both world models are removed and the policy acts in a single forward pass. Across five held-out evaluation scenarios, this yields navigation that is simultaneously safer, more socially and group-coherent, and more goal-directed than strong reactive and learned baselines, with naive observers rating the resulting motion as more human-like. Taken together, our findings point to a different approach for bringing world-model foresight into deployable agents: not accelerating rollouts, but amortizing them into the policy so that the cost of looking ahead is confined to training. Project page: this https URL

Abstract:Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. 75. 【2611.07713】Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: this https URL.

77. 【2611.07709】AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709

作者：Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Richard Chen,Angela Yao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：view synthesis, leading approach, approach for photorealistic, photorealistic novel view, Gaussian Splatting

备注： Accpeted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: [this https URL](https://eldentse.github.io/AffineGS/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the leading approach for photorealistic novel view synthesis, yet its geometric accuracy lags behind its visual quality. Existing depth-guided methods attempt to resolve this by regularizing the optimization with monocular depth priors, typically using a single global scale-and-shift alignment. Crucially, we discover a fundamental flaw in this assumption: modern monocular depth networks systematically exhibit region-dependent, piecewise-linear distortions. This discovery invalidates standard global alignment strategies, which warp scene geometry by enforcing a single transform. To address this, we introduce a spatially varying affine rectification model that corrects monocular disparity into consistent metric depth using regionally smooth, edge-aware fields. We further derive a normal-aware regularizer that uses spatial gradients to couple the rectified disparity with 3DGS depth and normals. Across diverse experimental settings, our sensor-free method shows strong photometric quality of Gaussian Splatting while substantially improving geometric accuracy and outperforming state-of-the art geometry-focused methods.

78. 【2611.07705】FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705

作者：Laurenz Ruzicka,Roman Pflugfelder,Daniel Cremers

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：long-range target tracking, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, motion remains informative, airspace monitoring, small Unmanned Aerial

备注： NeurIPS 2026, [this https URL](https://neurips.cc/virtual/2026/loc/sydney/poster/138996)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Optical flow is an important cue for drone analysis in aerial surveillance, airspace monitoring, and long-range target tracking, where motion remains informative when appearance is weak. Estimating the motion of small Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, however, is difficult because distant targets occupy few pixels, have low contrast, and move against complex dynamic backgrounds. Standard optical-flow benchmarks emphasize large, object-centric motions and therefore do not characterize distant aerial targets well. We introduce FlyingDrones, a generated benchmark with dense ground-truth optical flow and segmentation masks derived from physically based rendering, and define resolution-normalized metrics (EPE Norm) that normalize flow by image dimensions. We evaluate 16 state-of-the-art model families on a motion- and size-stratified subset of 999 flow pairs at three resolutions. Every family recovers drone motion better than the zero-flow reference, but the gap between families widens with resolution: at 1920x1080, global-matching, recurrent, transformer, and short-window temporal models separate clearly from pyramid, classical, self-supervised, lightweight, and diffusion models. Within-family checkpoint spread is comparable to or larger than between-family spread, so the training curriculum is as important as the architecture. Two complementary studies test the benchmark beyond synthetic accuracy: a ground-truth-free motion-alignment evaluation on 21 cameras of real UAV footage shows that the synthetic ranking carries a moderate, significant signal about real-world performance (Spearman \r{ho} = 0.63 over 41 checkpoints) while the exact top ranks differ, and a fine-tuning study shows that 800 FlyingDrones pairs reduce held-out drone-region error by up to 37% and improve drone localization on the real footage for all four architectures tested.

79. 【2611.07702】InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702

作者：Liangjian Wen,Qun Dai,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Fuyuan Cao,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：exploit synergistic information, expected to exploit, exploit synergistic, multiple modalities, synergistic information

备注： Accepted for publication in IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence (TPAMI). 16 pages; supplementary material provided as an ancillary file. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at this https URL.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673

作者：Raúl Boix-Aldeguer,Manuel Benavent-Lledo,David Ortiz-Perez,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez

备注： 9 pages, 6 figures. Under review at IEEE FG 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Action recognition, and more specifically egocentric action recognition, is a core task in computer vision, with applications such as monitoring the daily life activities of elderly people and task assistance. Recent methods rely on visual modalities such as RGB video or depth. However, in egocentric video, an action is largely defined by its objects, which these methods rarely model. We propose a method that combines a frozen SlowFast video expert with an attention-based object expert by late fusion. To remove them, the same vision-language model generates an interaction affordance that decides whether a hand is manipulating each detected object. On MECCANO, objects raise the Top-1 of the video expert from 44.9% to 49.0%, and the two affordances raise it to 49.9%, above SlowFast and object-aware RGB baselines. Code will be released upon acceptance.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: this https URL.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662

作者：Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：Lifelong object maps, Lifelong object, leaves view, Lifelong, object

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658

作者：Minh Canh Tu,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：casual images poses, remain mutually consistent, assets are essential, simulation and training, key challenge

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Post-training aims to enhance desired attributes in visual generative models while preserving their general generation capabilities. Reward-guided post-training faces two challenges: real training data may mismatch the generator's output distribution, and attribute supervision may be confounded by unrelated content. Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this paper, we introduce Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector (UniGADD), a novel method that employs supervised learning to achieve high accuracy on known deepfake generators, while maintaining robust performance on previously unseen ones. This refinement enhances generalisability, enabling the method to exhibit improved robustness against unseen deepfake generation techniques. UniGADD achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art methods for known generators, while significantly outperforming them on unseen cases, demonstrating its scalability and practical applicability for adversarial content detection.

Abstract:Recent advances in diffusion distillation have enabled high-quality image generation in only one or a few network function evaluations (NFEs). 93. 【2611.07593】ACT-KV: Tri-Axis Cosine Transform for Compressing Volumetric KV Caches in Medical VLMs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07593

作者：Chongyu Qu,Ritchie Zhao,Yufan He,Dong Yang,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Juming Xiong,Junlin Guo,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Daguang Xu,Bennett Allan Landman,Yucheng Tang,Yuankai Huo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：structural memory wall, Vision-language models, face a structural, medical scans face, MRI volume produces

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models (VLMs) applied to 3D medical scans face a structural memory wall. A single CT or MRI volume produces tens of thousands of vision tokens, and the resulting key-value (KV) cache scales linearly with both batch and context. Existing KV compression methods operate on the cache's feature or token statistics, or apply 1D and 2D transforms, leaving the three-axis spatial structure in the cache unexploited. We propose TACT-KV (Tri-Axis Cosine Transform), a calibration-free KV compression method that exploits strong spatial correlations in visual KV states along all three anatomical axes of 3D scans. This correlation concentrates the KV cache energy under a frequency-domain transform. TACT-KV applies a separable 3D discrete cosine transform to the KV cache, allocates a per-layer bit budget across frequency bins via reverse water-filling (more bits to high-energy components), and quantizes each bin with a fixed scalar codebook. We derive a close-form relation: the distortion reduction from adaptive bit allocation is dominated by the spectral concentration of the transformed cache. Across six medical VLMs and two 3D CT benchmarks, TACT-KV achieves the lowest 1-bit prediction-flip rate on eleven of twelve model and dataset pairs in our main comparison. The same advantage reproduces on four general-purpose VLMs evaluated on video tasks, indicating that the benefit of exploiting three-axis structure generalizes beyond medical imaging. At equal high-bandwidth memory (HBM), TACT-KV scales the decoding batch size on a single GPU by up to $8\times$.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Out-of-Distribution (OoD) detection is pivotal for reliable deployments of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), aiming to distinguish anomalous samples from the In-Distribution (InD) data in training. By introducing outliers as surrogate OoD data to finetune DNNs, Outlier Exposure (OE) has greatly advanced OoD detection in recent years. Existing OE-based research remains predominantly logit-centric, either by generating diverse outliers or by regularizing them with objectives operating at the prediction-output level. Nonetheless, these approaches leave much of the rich information in feature spaces untapped. In this work, we propose an OE-based detection framework, named RecOE, that learns appropriate feature subspaces from InD data and auxiliary outliers, such that the InD-OoD discrepancy can be effectively characterized through the learned feature subspaces. Specifically, the feature subspaces are optimized to capture the dominant variations of InD and outliers by minimizing reconstruction errors therein. Besides, a sample-level objective discourages reconstruction in the opposing subspace, while an orthogonality regularizer reduces subspace overlap, further promoting discriminative subspaces. At inference, the difference in reconstruction errors between the learned subspaces serves as the OoD detection score. Extensive experiments show the superiority of RecOE on multiple benchmarks, in which significant performance gains are achieved over recent strong baselines with only slight extra overhead from two small-size matrices of subspace bases. The source code is available at this https URL.

95. 【2611.07575】Georeferenced Cross-View 3D Reconstruction from Ground and Satellite Images

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07575

作者：David G. Shatwell,Mubarak Shah

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：geographic coordinate frame, images requires recovering, local coordinate frame, coordinate frame, local scene geometry

备注： Accepted in NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Georeferenced 3D reconstruction from ground images requires recovering not only the local scene geometry, but also localizing every camera center and 3D point in a geographic coordinate frame. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods are able to estimate the GPS coordinate of a single ground camera, but do not reconstruct dense 3D geometry, while recent feed-forward multi-view 3D reconstruction models predict point maps and camera poses only in a local coordinate frame. We introduce X-VGGT, a feed-forward cross-view geometry transformer that unifies these tasks by jointly processing a set of ground-level images and a georeferenced satellite image to predict ground-view camera poses, dense 3D point maps, and a scene-level transform parameterized by gravity, scale, yaw and translation. This transform projects the local reconstruction into the satellite plane, resulting in the geo-registration of all ground images with the georeferenced satellite image. We further propose an evaluation protocol for this joint task and show that, across multiple cross-view datasets, X-VGGT produces accurate georeferenced reconstructions and outperforms prior cross-view geo-localization and multi-view 3D reconstruction models.

96. 【2611.07573】Integrating Learned Priors through Prior-Space Reparameterization for Physically Based Inverse Rendering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07573

作者：Hun Hwangbo,Minjun Park,Yongsung Kim,Wooseok Song,Jungmin Ko,Junghyun Hwang,Sungroh Yoon

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Physically based inverse rendering decomposes appearance into materials and illumination, an ambiguous problem for which learned material predictions provide useful prior information. Most existing methods incorporate these cues through auxiliary regularization losses, which alter the optimization objective and require balancing their influence against photometric fitting. We propose a prior-guided material reparameterization that uses the predicted feature relationships to restrict the optimization space while leaving the objective unchanged. We fuse multi-view material predictions onto a mesh and construct a permutohedral lattice in the resulting prior feature space. Surface points with similar predictions share material variables, which are optimized jointly with geometry and illumination using a purely photometric objective. Experiments with two learned predictors on three synthetic benchmarks and Stanford-ORB demonstrate improved material decomposition and relighting. Our method improves relighting PSNR by 1.5-2.5 dB over the strongest evaluated mesh-based baseline on Synthetic4Relight and TensoIR-Synthetic, and outperforms a regularization loss using the same prior in controlled comparisons. We further show that our reparameterization pools noisy photometric gradients, producing updates that are more consistent across sampled views. This consistency enables a shortened joint optimization schedule that takes less than a minute while retaining relighting quality above all evaluated baselines on TensoIR-Synthetic.

97. 【2611.07566】Guidance Is a Field, Not a Scalar: Interval-Integrated Classifier-Free Guidance for One-Step Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07566

作者：Zijie Meng,Xixin Cao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：One-step generators built, single function evaluation, generators built, inherit a rigid, rigid treatment

备注： 10 pages, 9 figures. Accepted to WACV 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:One-step generators built on MeanFlow synthesize a video in a single function evaluation, yet inherit a rigid treatment of classifier-free guidance (CFG): the guidance scale is frozen during training or, at best, exposed as a single scalar. This conflicts with mounting evidence that the optimal guidance is not a constant but a field that varies with the noise level, with spatial content, and, for video, with frame position. Every existing remedy presupposes a multi-step sampler along which guidance can be re-scheduled--an assumption one-step models violate by construction. We resolve this tension through a single observation: because the MeanFlow target is an integral over a noise interval, an arbitrary guidance field can be absorbed into the target itself and compiled into one evaluation. We formalize this as the interval-integrated CFG identity, prove that the compiled guidance carries no schedule-discretization error and can thus match or exceed any finite multi-step sampler, and instantiate it as AGAVE, which equips the average-velocity field with a learnable temporal schedule, a free motion-saliency spatial modulation, and a frame-position asymmetry. Adding fewer than ten scalars at zero inference cost, AGAVE surpasses all one-step baselines on VBench, UCF-101 and MSR-VTT while matching multi-step guided sampling. Project repository: this https URL.

100. 【2611.07551】raining-Free Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning for Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07551

作者：Jia Li,Xurui Peng,Xing Wang,Hayden Kwok-Hay So

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 19 pages, 10 figures, 11 tables, including appendices

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk and have made efficient video generation practical. However, further accelerating these models toward real-time generation remains challenging, as few-step distillation leaves little cross-step redundancy for existing training-free caching methods to exploit. We revisit this regime and identify two token-level redundancy patterns: cross-frame redundancy from static content shared across adjacent frames, and cross-step redundancy from tokens that converge early during denoising. Crucially, we find that early clean estimates along the model's own denoising trajectory provide an effective online signal for locating both patterns, requiring no retraining, auxiliary predictor, or reference video. Based on these findings, we propose PreSTP (Predictive Spatio-Temporal Token Pruning), a training-free framework that constructs a unified token-wise redundancy mask from cross-frame similarity and cross-step convergence. Tokens marked as redundant skip subsequent computation under a consistent last-computed-value restoration rule, and a content-adaptive mask reuse strategy further reduces dense probing cost. On 5-second generation, PreSTP achieves 1.52x-3.20x speedups and a better efficiency-fidelity trade-off than coarse-grained caching baselines such as TeaCache and FlowCache. On 30-second generation, it reaches up to 1.96x speedup while improving VBench-Long and suppressing long-horizon drift. Notably, it continues to reduce inference cost even in the extreme one-step regime, where cross-step redundancy vanishes entirely and coarse step-wise caching is no longer applicable.

101. 【2611.07543】OptIMerge: Optimization-Based Merging of Diffusion Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07543

作者：Viraj Shah,Ashwini Pokle,Peyman Milanfar,Mauricio Delbracio,Hossein Talebi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multiple task-specific models, shared base model, task-specific models fine-tuned, multiple task-specific, shared base

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The arithmetic merging of weights from multiple task-specific models fine-tuned from a shared base model, commonly known as model souping, has emerged as a popular strategy for multi-task learning without the computational overhead of joint retraining. However, naive scalar weight averaging frequently leads to severe performance degradation when the constituent models learn structurally divergent feature representations, leading to destructive parameter interference. We study this problem in a largely unexplored setting: merging image-to-image (I2I) diffusion models fine-tuned for dense, pixel-accurate tasks such as image restoration. Unlike the large language models (LLMs) and text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models for which most merging methods were developed, I2I experts encode conflicting spatial priors (e.g., anisotropic motion-blur vs. isotropic defocus-blur) in their convolutional filters, where both naive soup and LLM-style element-wise merging introduce visible artifacts. We introduce OptIMerge, a gradient-based optimization framework: instead of relying on heuristic scalars, OptIMerge learns highly expressive, yet parameter-efficient merger coefficients by minimizing a multi-task diffusion loss over a small set of 100-500 images. OptIMerge parameterizes the merge with architecture-aware transformations: for attention layers, a structured low-rank feature alignment with explicit channel biases; for convolutional layers, per-kernel merger coefficients, and a low-rank Cayley transform that aligns channels by orthogonal rotation without distorting the filters. By keeping the experts frozen and optimizing only these lightweight merger parameters (equivalent to 0.4% of the network footprint), OptIMerge achieves simultaneous multi-task capabilities that rival computationally expensive joint training and decisively outperform zero-shot model soups.

103. 【2611.07513】Concertina: Embodying Manipulation and Cognition in One Diffusion Vision-Language Model

Abstract:Most vision-language-action (VLA) policies decouple manipulation from cognition by bolting an external action mechanism onto a vision-language model (VLM)--either quantizing actions into discrete tokens whose errors bear no stable correspondence to physical displacements, or delegating continuous trajectory generation to a separate action expert whose gradients compete with or are blocked from updating the VLM backbone. 104. 【2611.07512】RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Decision-based sparse attacks must reduce perturbation support using only the victim's predicted label. The best restoration strategy varies as support contracts: coarse removal works early, while tail states need finer, history-aware tests. We propose RLGPA, an online reinforcement learning method that schedules support-reduction executors and their granularity under a hard query budget. A masked Dueling Double DQN selects among seven stable policy--scale actions; each action produces support-relative restoration groups that are rebuilt after every macro decision. State-blind and state-shuffled controls degrade significantly, confirming that the learned policy uses attack state; the main gains come from state-conditioned executor scheduling and dynamic repartition. 105. 【2611.07502】Coverage Hides Churn: Auditing and Preserving Conformal Decisions as VLM Label Registries Grow

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07502

作者：Jai Kumar Sharma,Peeyush Tapadiya

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Vision-language classifiers, serve keep growing, classifiers can learn, lists they serve, Vision-language

备注： Accepted at WACV 2027. Includes supplementary material

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language classifiers can learn new labels from text alone, so the label lists they serve keep growing. We show that this growth silently changes conformal decisions about old labels. Common softmax-based conformal scores compare each label with an average over all labels; adding labels changes that average, and with it the score of every old label, even when the model, the image, and the old calibration data stay the same. Recalibrating after the update keeps the coverage guarantee, yet across eleven settings it changes at least one old decision on 53-90% of new images. Coverage cannot reveal this, because it checks only the true label: in five controlled settings that keep the old calibration, so that only the normalizer changes, old-class coverage moves by at most 0.01 while 60-67% of images change. We propose a lifecycle audit that measures these changes alongside coverage and set size, and a simple fix, PrefixCP: each class keeps the label registry that existed when it was added. Its old decisions then never change, each class keeps exact class-conditional coverage under exchangeability, and one prefix scan computes all historical normalizers at once. PrefixCP recovers 40.9-58.5% of the set-size gap between never updating the reference and recalibrating, which gives smaller sets but changes old decisions.

106. 【2611.07498】Degree-Equalized View-Graph Selection under Tight Pair Budgets

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07498

作者：Minhyeok Im,Jongseong Brad Choi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：practical pipelines pass, practical pipelines, pipelines pass, pass a budgeted, budgeted subset

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Fully matching all image pairs in Structure-from-Motion is quadratic in the number of images, so practical pipelines pass a budgeted subset to the final matching and reconstruction backend. One-shot selectors score candidates with image retrieval, cheap geometric probes, or a learned prior and finalize B pairs before full matching. Their priorities do not account for the degree already allocated to each image, which can concentrate edges on a few images. Across nine large 1DSfM internet photo collections at B=2N, the coverage top-B selector leaves 54% of covered images with realized degree at most two. Reconstruction can then fail even when the selected graph is nearly connected and almost fully verified. Our training-free greedy rule, Degree-Equalized Allocation (DEAL), exponentially downweights pairs whose endpoints are already well covered. On these collections, DEAL improves the one-shot selector built on the same prior (from 0.776 to 0.929) and matches the mean registered-image ratio of an SfM-supervised edge prior under the shared backend (0.929 vs 0.930), without training. On the largely saturated 38-scene standard benchmark suite, DEAL gives the highest mean among the evaluated training-free selectors (0.875 vs 0.858 one-shot).
