109. 【2611.07443】VIPA: View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07443

作者：Jiyu Han,Qingyue Deng,Bingyao Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：produce incorrect predictions, causing deep image, Projector-based adversarial attack, Projector-based adversarial, deep image classifiers

备注： 16 pages, 12 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Projector-based adversarial attack aims to physically manipulate real-world scenes by projecting adversarial patterns, thereby causing deep image classifiers to produce incorrect predictions. However, existing stealthy projector-based adversarial attack methods model the project-and-capture process in a single and static view, limiting their view-invariant capability and practical applicability in dynamic environments. In this paper, we introduce View-Invariant Projector-Based Adversarial Attack (VIPA), a novel method designed to overcome these limitations by achieving both view-invariant and classifier-agnostic adversarial attacks in the physical world. We first leverage a view-invariant projector-camera system simulation method to model the physical interactions between projected patterns and real-world surfaces. To ensure robustness and stealthiness of the attack across different views, VIPA optimizes adversarial projections by aggregating simulated attack losses from multiple views. This joint optimization across diverse views helps maintain robustness regardless of the viewpoints. Finally, the optimized patterns are physically projected into real-world scenes, where they successfully fool various classifiers from different viewpoints, thereby enabling robust and practical view-invariant adversarial attacks. Our experiments in both targeted and untargeted attacks demonstrate that VIPA consistently achieves higher attack success rates than existing methods, while also enhancing stealthiness and ensuring minimal perceptual degradation across all tested views.

111. 【2611.07431】RiST: Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer for Medical Image Analysis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07431

作者：Pengfei Gu,Huimin Li,Guangyu Meng,Hao Zheng,Haoteng Tang,Bin Fu,Danny Z. Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：hierarchical Vision Transformers, components and loops, organ boundaries, medical image analysis, connected components

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical images contain topological patterns, such as connected components and loops, at scales ranging from small lesions to organ boundaries. Although hierarchical Vision Transformers have advanced medical image analysis, they do not explicitly incorporate topology throughout their multiscale representations. We propose Topology-Reinforced Swin Transformer (TRiST), a framework that integrates topological information into feature construction, refinement, and attention. First, a Hierarchical Multi-Scale Topology Representation algorithm computes 2D persistent homology (H0 and H1) independently at four native Swin stages, producing descriptors aligned with each stage's token grid rather than downsampling fine-scale topology. Second, a Band-Aware H0/H1 Residual Refinement module uses gated residual networks to process short-, medium-, and long-persistence information separately. Third, a Persistence-Band Progressive Attention Bias converts stage-specific descriptors into learned key-saliency scores that directly modify shifted-window attention logits. We instantiate TRiST as Topo-SwinV2-B for classification and Topo-Swin-UNet for segmentation, preserving the underlying Swin-based backbones. Experiments on five medical image datasets show consistent improvements over Swin-based baselines and competitive performance relative to existing topology-augmented methods.

作者：Yuze Lu

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： 15 pages, 12 figures. Preprint; supplementary material and code to be released upon formal publication

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Robots increasingly run large vision-language-action policies on remote servers, making the camera uplink the bottleneck. Existing video codecs spend bits on content the receiver could predict: the robot's own motion. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:Single image dehazing requires recovering scene radiance from visual responses that are weakened and veiled by participating media. Existing methods either invert explicit scattering variables, which can be unstable under complex scenes, or learn direct restoration mappings, which often discard the physical organization of haze formation. We present \emph{FreePath}, a neural mean free path framework for image dehazing. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:Dance motion combines slowly varying body configurations with rapid local movements. We investigate how explicit temporal-frequency decomposition can guide the temporal resolution and codebook capacity of discrete motion representations. We introduce frequency-factorized motion tokenization (FFMT), which separates root-relative motion into a low-frequency choreographic scaffold and a complementary high-frequency residual. FFMT uses a lower token rate and a smaller codebook for the low-frequency component, and a higher token rate and a larger codebook for the high-frequency residual. Abstract:High-quality material generation for physically based rendering (PBR) remains challenging because datasets and synthesis pipelines lack standardized representations. 122. 【2611.07294】Householder Motion Embedding: Noise Modulation for Motion-Controllable Video Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07294

作者：Tao Jun Lin,Jinhong Ni,Ruyi Zha,Yifu Wang,Xibin Song,Pan Ji,Hongdong Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：achieved remarkable success, guidance remains challenging, explicit user guidance, user guidance remains, precise motion control

备注： NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion models for video generation have achieved remarkable success, yet precise motion control under explicit user guidance remains challenging. Recent noise-warping methods introduce temporal correlations by warping the noise according to the user-provided motion signal. However, standard spatial warping generally disrupts the noise's i.i.d. Gaussian structure, requiring distribution correction, such as Jacobian-based correction, but at a substantial computational overhead. We propose Householder Motion Embedding (HME), which encodes the motion signal into the noise through a sequence of Householder reflections that together form an orthogonal transformation applied to each location's channel vector. HME exactly preserves the spatial Gaussian structure within each frame while introducing cross-frame correlations that promote temporally consistent video. HME is agnostic to the network architecture and integrates directly into standard video diffusion fine-tuning pipelines. Experiments on different scenarios show that HME matches or outperforms the state of the art, while achieving a 2.7x speedup over noise warping. These results demonstrate that channel-space Householder transformations offer a principled and efficient alternative to noise warping for motion-controllable video generation.

Abstract:Radiologists read a screening mammogram by first forming a global impression of the four views and then selectively magnifying suspicious regions before assigning a BI-RADS assessment. MLLMs, in contrast, see the whole image at a fixed, reduced resolution, which makes sub-millimeter calcifications invisible, and they perform close to chance on mammography. 124. 【2611.07176】VINO: Revisiting 3D Representation Learning with Volume Transformers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07176

作者：Adrian Kruse,Kadir Yilmaz,Daan de Geus,Francis Engelmann,Bastian Leibe

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：achieved comparable success, vision foundation models, Self-supervised learning, downstream tasks, comparable success

备注： Project page at: [this http URL](http://vision.rwth-aachen.de/VINO)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-supervised learning has given rise to vision foundation models that learn general-purpose image representations transferable across downstream tasks, yet representation learning for 3D scenes has not achieved comparable success. Motivated by this, we identify key limitations of current 3D representation learning approaches and revisit their core design choices. We first replace the commonly used hierarchical PTv3 backbone with the Volume Transformer, a ViT-like architecture for 3D scenes. We then redesign two core objectives of modern approaches built around student-teacher networks: matching representations across views and predicting masked content from visible context. For the first objective, we separate the roles of teacher and student: the teacher provides clean, consistent targets while the student learns invariance to stronger perturbations. For the second objective, we prevent the student from accessing the masked geometry and instead require it to infer missing representations from visible context only. Finally, we redesign 2D-to-3D knowledge distillation, replacing direct regression of view-dependent image features with view-consistent supervision. Together, these changes form VINO, a framework that learns semantic representations of 3D scenes without labels. VINO substantially improves dense linear probing across indoor and outdoor benchmarks and transfers strongly to out-of-domain settings.

125. 【2611.07148】Perceptual Distortion for Overfitted Video Coding: Sliced Matching in Space, Error Alignment in Time

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07148

作者：Tianyi Yang,Ge Gao,Fan Zhang,David Bull

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recent works, fixed bitrate, works in neural, increasingly been trading, trading off perceptual

备注： 5 pages, 4 figures; submitted to IEEE ISCAS

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent works in neural video coding have increasingly been trading off perceptual quality against reconstruction fidelity at a fixed bitrate. Wasserstein Distortion (WD) offers a principled approach to balancing these objectives by relaxing spatial correspondences in feature matching, enabling lightweight overfitted image codecs to compete with computationally intensive generative decoders. Extending vanilla WD to video, however, presents two challenges: its independent Gaussian approximation limits the expressiveness of feature distribution matching, while unconstrained texture resampling across frames introduces temporal inconsistencies. We address these challenges by distinguishing between the correspondences that can be relaxed and those that must be preserved. Spatially, we introduce a local sliced Wasserstein distortion (SWD) that matches empirical feature distributions through one-dimensional optimal transport, capturing cross-channel dependencies without enforcing exact spatial alignment. Temporally, we employ a novel flow residual that constrains coding errors along motion trajectories, preserving temporal consistency without imposing additional reconstruction fidelity constraints. Evaluated on UVG and HEVC Class B with the overfitted codec COOL-CHIC, our framework achieves BD-rate reductions of 9.2% in DISTS and 3.0% in LPIPS relative to frame-wise WD, averaged over test sequences, while lowering tOF by 10.2% at matched rate.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07081

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-perspective-camera systems are widely adopted since they provide broad visual coverage for autonomous driving, motivating the synthesis of a unified wide view from spatially separated observations. In this work, we formulate wide view synthesis (WVS), which aims to synthesize a geometrically consistent wide view at a specified virtual viewpoint from multiple perspective cameras. Unlike conventional image stitching, WVS requires reasoning about 3D geometry and visibility to handle depth-dependent parallax and occlusions arising from spatially separated camera centers. While vanilla 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity view synthesis, its reliance on per-scene optimization limits its applicability to driving scenes. Furthermore, WVS introduces additional challenges, *e.g.*, peripheral stretching under perspective projection and photometric inconsistencies across cameras. Specifically, we introduce Piecewise Perspective Rendering (PPR) to mitigate peripheral stretching through local perspective projections sharing a common camera center, and Spatial Affine Photometric Modeling (SAPM) to account for camera-dependent photometric variations during training. We further introduce **WideDrive**, a synthetic benchmark providing dense ground-truth wide views and adapt a LiDAR-projected sparse-reference protocol to real-world datasets. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of WV-GS for WVS. Dataset and code will be publicly released.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Few-shot adaptation of vision-language models must balance two competing goals: learning fine-grained visual distinctions from very limited supervision and preserving the efficiency that makes frozen CLIP-like models attractive for deployment. Existing lightweight adaptation methods, including prompt tuning, LoRA, and cache-based adapters, typically supervise global image embeddings and therefore underuse token-level structure. Conversely, structure-aware methods exploit local evidence but often introduce additional inference-time computation. We propose SD-LoRA, a structural distillation framework that uses token-level graph reasoning only during training and transfers its effects into a lightweight LoRA-adapted CLIP model. During training, a heterogeneous graph teacher models patch-patch, global-local, and visual-text relations over high-resolution ViT tokens. At inference, the graph teacher is discarded; prediction requires only a single CLIP-LoRA forward pass and a cache-based classifier. Code is available at this https URL.

Abstract:Skin lesion segmentation without annotated masks requires the target to be specified through other cues. Existing approaches rely on dermoscopy-specific rules, annotation-derived model priors, or user-selected regions. A general text-to-image diffusion transformer offers a text interface to the target, but its concept-word attention is unreliable on dermoscopy images: in the model's attention-output space, the word-derived skin-to-lesion direction is poorly aligned with the direction separating real skin from real lesions. We show that the direction derived from generated healthy-skin and lesion images is substantially better aligned. Based on this finding, we propose GENIQ, a training-free method that constructs initial skin and lesion prototypes from generated images and refines them using the most skin-like and lesion-like patches from unlabelled dermoscopy images. It scores test patches along the refined direction and applies a gated threshold to suppress contextual spill-over around small lesions. Without inference-time annotations or additional models, GENIQ achieves Dice scores of 0.85-0.88 on three dermoscopy test sets. It outperforms zero-shot and word-based attention methods on all three sets, and a hand-designed dermoscopy pipeline in Dice and IoU on two.

130. 【2611.07055】Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055

作者：Wenxuan Peng,Richard Zhang,Haitian Zheng,Jaskirat Singh,Tsai-Shien Chen,Eli Shechtman,Bharath Hariharan,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Zongze Wu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Flow matching, generators often face, Flow, quality-coverage tradeoff, Recall point

备注： 40 pages, 24 figures. Project page: [this https URL](https://lilydaytoy.github.io/CFM/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Flow matching (FM) generators often face a quality-coverage tradeoff. Inference-time techniques such as classifier-free guidance raise quality, but at the cost of coverage. We hypothesize this limitation stems from a basic property of the objective: an intermediate noisy image could correspond to many clean images, yet the model can only predict a single velocity, at best averaging over the multiple possibilities. To mitigate this, we introduce competition among predictions during training. We achieve this by augmenting the predictor with a randomly drawn low-dimensional conditioning code $z$. For each training example, we sample $K$ values of $z$ to produce $K$ candidate velocities that compete to explain the target, and apply the loss only to the best-matching candidate. This winner-take-all objective allows different modes to shape different predictions, rather than being averaged into a single compromise. Furthermore, this term naturally enables a new form of guidance, auxiliary conditioning scaling, which complements traditional guidance methods. Together, these two guidance mechanisms show that our method substantially improves the quality-coverage frontier. We validate on ImageNet-256, using two different testbeds, SiT and RAEv2. Given less than 3% of the original SiT pretraining compute for post-training, at the 67% Recall point, our method reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 19.22 to 5.91. On RAEv2, at the 70% Recall point, it reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 16.83 to 3.18.

131. 【2611.07049】Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049

作者：Yiyan Wang,Haohan Liu,Tetsuo Shibuya

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：combines compact textured, compact textured surfaces, rendering combines compact, surfaces with flexible, offering an efficient

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027. 16 pages including supplementary material, 11 figures, 8 tables. Project page: [this https URL](https://ooosn.github.io/gaussians-can-subtract/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian rendering combines compact textured surfaces with flexible 3D Gaussian primitives, offering an efficient alternative to purely Gaussian scene representations for structured environments. Existing scene-reconstruction hybrids commonly follow an additive formulation, where Gaussian residuals compensate for errors left by the mesh. However, not every Mesh-Gaussian conflict is caused by missing content: residual geometry errors, occlusion mismatch, texture-projection artifacts, and view-dependent appearance can make the same mesh content consistent in one view but inconsistent in another. We therefore formulate hybrid rendering as a multi-view inconsistency problem and present Gaussolve, a subtract-and-refill framework that selectively dissolves inconsistent mesh content before residual Gaussians refill the revealed regions. We introduce a lightweight Neural Dissolve Field, parameterized by scalar spherical-harmonic Gaussians, to learn where and from which viewing directions mesh content should be dissolved. A continuous transmittance fusion rule progressively exposes residual Gaussians behind the dissolved mesh, preserving their reconstruction gradients and avoiding hard visibility discontinuities. Experiments on structured and semi-structured scenes demonstrate higher visual fidelity with fewer Gaussian primitives and faster rendering. Project page: this https URL

132. 【2611.07009】raCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009

作者：Wooil Lee,Junpyo Hong,Jongseo Lee,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tsui-Wei Weng,Jinwoo Choi

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Explainable Video Understanding, existing video XAI, Video understanding, explanations can obscure, Video understanding models

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: [this https URL](https://leewooil.github.io/TraCE_project_page/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video understanding models often rely on motion, yet their explanations can obscure which motion sources support a prediction. We identify motion-source under-specification as a limitation of existing video XAI: region-centric explanations can be source-ambiguous, while motion vocabularies restricted to single-person pose sequences are source-incomplete. We propose TraCE (Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding), a concept-based framework that discovers source-flexible motion concepts by clustering optical-flow trajectories from training videos. These concepts represent dynamic evidence from people, objects, and interactions, while object and scene concepts account for static context. The resulting typed trajectory/object/scene interface supports class prediction for action recognition and question-conditioned concept matching for video question answering (VQA). We evaluate TraCE across five random seeds on six benchmarks: NTU RGB+D Mutual, Chiral SSv2, UCF-101, KTH, TOMATO, and STAR. Relative to non-interpretable baselines, TraCE maintains competitive task performance, with an average accuracy drop of 1.0 points for action recognition and a nominal gain of 0.4 points for VQA. Motion perturbation and concept-removal tests provide complementary evidence of directional semantic alignment and decision faithfulness, while sample-level interventions illustrate targeted model debugging. Our project page is available at this https URL.

133. 【2611.06993】Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993

作者：Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Amr Ahmed

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：improves vision-language models, current methods face, adaptation improves vision-language, key trade-off, transductive VLM adaptation

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transductive adaptation improves vision-language models at test time by refining predictions on an unlabeled batch, but current methods face a key trade-off. Aggressive approaches exploit batch structure but can drift when batches are sparse, have prior shift, or cover little of the label space. Conservative methods better preserve CLIP text semantics, but fixed semantic references can under-adapt even when the batch provides strong corrective evidence. We propose JPTA (Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation), a prior-aware framework that casts transductive VLM adaptation as batch-conditioned semantic reference estimation. Instead of treating CLIP text prototypes as fixed priors or freely replaceable, JPTA views them as semantic references that shift toward image-side batch structure only under calibrated evidence. It estimates batch priors and soft image-side prototypes to form transported semantic references whose impact is scaled by batch reliability. Across standard benchmarks, low effective-class regimes, all-class evaluation, and online streams, JPTA consistently outperforms TransCLIP and StatA, with the largest gains on sparse and prior-shifted batches. Anchor-drift, active-support, and absent-class-mass diagnostics show that JPTA fixes effective-class mismatch without unconstrained transductive drift, indicating that robust transductive VLM adaptation should recalibrate semantic references using reliable batch evidence rather than choose between unrestricted transduction and fixed prototypes. Code is available at this https URL.

134. 【2611.06974】$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974

作者：Zhihao Tang,Youjia Zhang,Mingbo Zhao,Wei Yang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：Triangle-based splatting connects, conventional graphics pipelines, Triangle-based splatting, requires excessive tessellation, preserve appearance details

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Triangle-based splatting connects novel view synthesis with conventional graphics pipelines, but often requires excessive tessellation to preserve appearance details. We present T$^2$-Splat, which decouples topology adaptation from appearance refinement. Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) initializes mesh connectivity. Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) then adapts triangle allocation through edge collapse and vertex split operations. Residual per-face Mip-textures then refine appearance on the fixed mesh using scale-adaptive filtering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive results with compact meshes. T$^2$-Splat improves PSNR by 0.71dB over MeshSplatting on Mip-NeRF 360. Additional compression experiments demonstrate flexible control of mesh complexity, supporting face budgets as low as 10% of the original face count. Project page: this https URL.

135. 【2611.06255】Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255

作者：Hongeun Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：flow-based generative models, alignment for diffusion, stochastic trajectories, Initial noise optimization, flow-based generative

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

136. 【2611.08359】A Theoretical Analysis of a Bilevel Optimization Formulation for Combined Local-Global Optical Flow Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08359

作者：El Mostafa Kalmoun

类目：Optimization and Control (math.OC); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：optical flow models, combined local-global, paper analyzes, analyzes a bilevel, bilevel formulation

备注： 31 pages, 5 figures. Submitted to the Journal of Mathematical Imaging and Vision. Python code in the ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper analyzes a bilevel formulation for learning the regularization parameter of combined local-global (CLG) optical flow models. The lower-level problem computes the flow from a Lucas--Kanade data term and a quadratic or total variation (TV) regularizer, and the upper-level problem selects the parameter that minimizes the squared distance to a ground-truth flow. The setting is finite-dimensional, and a full-rank assumption makes the lower-level solution unique. For the quadratic model we derive sufficient conditions for the existence of an optimal parameter, for every optimal parameter to be positive, and for strict local optimality. For TV regularization an optimal parameter always exists, because the lower-level solution vanishes for large parameters, and we give a sufficient condition for positivity that accounts for the coupling of the two flow components through the local structure tensors. Synthetic examples illustrate the conditions, including cases in which they fail.

137. 【2611.06935】An Evolutionary Hypothesis for White's Illusion: Constant Percentage Correction and Modern Individual Variation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06935

作者：Bekir Tevfik Akgün,Ergun Akleman

类目：Neurons and Cognition (q-bio.NC); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：striking demonstration, demonstration of context-dependent, context-dependent brightness, White illusion, producing approximately constant

备注： 12 pages and 7 figures. We may later send a shorter version to a conference

点击查看摘要

Abstract:White's illusion is a striking demonstration of context-dependent brightness and lightness perception. In earlier work, we developed a forward translucency-based formula capable of producing approximately constant perceived appearance across varying backgrounds. While validating that formulation, we observed a notable population-level pattern: individuals selected slightly different correction percentages for "constant perception," while the population average remained remarkably stable across experiments. In this paper, we propose an evolutionary hypothesis for this phenomenon. We suggest that a simple constant-percentage correction function could have emerged as an adaptive mechanism for maintaining useful material and reflectance judgments under variable illumination. Under this interpretation, the stability of the population mean may reflect a conserved perceptual mechanism, whereas individual differences may reflect learning, adaptation, or variation around a shared computational response. Using a modification of our previously published forward model, we show that White's illusion can be reproduced by applying a constant-percentage correction to background luminance. The model therefore provides a compact computational account of the observed population-level regularity and motivates a testable evolutionary interpretation of individual variation in contextual lightness judgments.

138. 【2611.06930】Category differences in similarity between fixation density maps and psychological potential fields for sketches

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06930

作者：Naoyuki Awano,Yuki Hayashi

类目：Neurons and Cognition (q-bio.NC); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：humans can instantaneously, instantaneously recognize, similarity, AUC, baseline-adjusted AUC

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:When an object comes into view, humans can instantaneously recognize it if known to them. However, assessing how this recognition takes place remains challenging. A few studies using simple sketches have reported varying degrees of image similarity between the fixation density map (FDM), which visualizes eye movements and durations, and the psychological potential field (PPF), which visualizes part of the visual cognition process, depending on the object category. This study reports how the similarity behaves across three categories---animal, non-animal, and meaningless shape---and under rotated presentation, which is known to increase cognitive load during object recognition. The similarity was quantified through area under the curve (AUC) measures, including a baseline-adjusted AUC that isolates the early rise in similarity from stimulus-dependent baseline levels. A variability score was introduced to assess individual differences, and linear mixed models were used to evaluate category and orientation effects. Results indicated that the baseline-adjusted AUC yielded significant differences among all three categories with large effect sizes, whereas the raw AUC did not distinguish between the animal and non-animal categories due to the influence of sketch representation. No significant Category $\times$ Orientation interaction was observed, indicating that rotated presentation did not produce a category-specific effect. The meaningless shape stimuli yielded consistently low similarity and variability scores, clearly separating them from the two meaningful object categories. These results characterize the behavior of the FDM--PPF similarity under free viewing and indicate that baseline adjustment is necessary to separate category-related variation from stimulus-dependent baseline levels.
